    'array[1..N] of int: student_advisor;   % Advisor for each student\n' +

    '\n' +
    '% Decision variables\n' +
    'array[1..J, 1..S, 1..N] of var bool: schedule; % schedule[j,s,n] = true if student n is scheduled in jury j, slot s\n' +
    'array[1..A, 1..J, 1..S] of var bool: advisor_present; % advisor_present[a,j,s] = true if advisor a is present in jury j, slot s\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% Constraints\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 1. Single defense per student - each student scheduled exactly once in their assigned jury\n' +
    'constraint forall(n in 1..N)(sum(s in 1..S)(schedule[student_jury[n], s, n]) = 1);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 2. Slot capacity - no more than C defenses per slot\n' +
    'constraint forall(j in 1..J, s in 1..S)(sum(n in 1..N)(schedule[j, s, n]) <= C);\n' +
    '\n' +
    "% 3. Advisor capacity - advisor must be present for their students' defenses\n" +
    'constraint forall(a in 1..A, j in 1..J, s in 1..S, n in 1..N where student_advisor[n] == a)(schedule[j, s, n] -> advisor_present[a, j, s]);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 4. Company incompatibility - incompatible companies cannot be in same slot\n' +
    'constraint forall(j in 1..J, s in 1..S, n1 in 1..N, n2 in 1..N where n1 < n2)(\n' +
    '    schedule[j, s, n1] /\\ \n' +
    '    schedule[j, s, n2] /\\ \n' +
    '    (exists(k in 1..num_incompatible_pairs)( \n' +
    '        (student_company[n1] == incompatible_pairs_c1[k] /\\ student_company[n2] == incompatible_pairs_c2[k]) \\/\n' +
    '        (student_company[n1] == incompatible_pairs_c2[k] /\\ student_company[n2] == incompatible_pairs_c1[k])\n' +
    '    )) -> false\n' +
    ');\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 5. Advisor availability - advisor can be in at most 2 slots per jury (more realistic)\n' +
    'constraint forall(a in 1..A, j in 1..J)(sum(s in 1..S)(advisor_present[a, j, s]) <= 2);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 6. Advisor exclusivity - advisor cannot be present in more than one jury at the same slot\n' +
    'constraint forall(a in 1..A, s in 1..S)(sum(j in 1..J)(advisor_present[a, j, s]) <= 1);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% Objective: Minimize total advisor presence (to reduce advisor workload)\n' +

    'array[1..A, 1..J, 1..S] of var bool: advisor_present; % advisor_present[a,j,s] = true if advisor a is present in jury j, slot s\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% Constraints\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 1. Single defense per student - each student scheduled exactly once in their assigned jury\n' +
    'constraint forall(n in 1..N)(sum(s in 1..S)(schedule[student_jury[n], s, n]) = 1);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 2. Slot capacity - no more than C defenses per slot\n' +
    'constraint forall(j in 1..J, s in 1..S)(sum(n in 1..N)(schedule[j, s, n]) <= C);\n' +
    '\n' +
    "% 3. Advisor capacity - advisor must be present for their students' defenses\n" +
    'constraint forall(a in 1..A, j in 1..J, s in 1..S, n in 1..N where student_advisor[n] == a)(schedule[j, s, n] -> advisor_present[a, j, s]);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 4. Company incompatibility - incompatible companies cannot be in same slot\n' +
    'constraint forall(j in 1..J, s in 1..S, n1 in 1..N, n2 in 1..N where n1 < n2)(\n' +
    '    schedule[j, s, n1] /\\ \n' +
    '    schedule[j, s, n2] /\\ \n' +
    '    (exists(k in 1..num_incompatible_pairs)( \n' +
    '        (student_company[n1] == incompatible_pairs_c1[k] /\\ student_company[n2] == incompatible_pairs_c2[k]) \\/\n' +
    '        (student_company[n1] == incompatible_pairs_c2[k] /\\ student_company[n2] == incompatible_pairs_c1[k])\n' +
    '    )) -> false\n' +
    ');\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 5. Advisor availability - advisor can be in at most 2 slots per jury (more realistic)\n' +
    'constraint forall(a in 1..A, j in 1..J)(sum(s in 1..S)(advisor_present[a, j, s]) <= 2);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 6. Advisor exclusivity - advisor cannot be present in more than one jury at the same slot\n' +
    'constraint forall(a in 1..A, s in 1..S)(sum(j in 1..J)(bool2int(advisor_present[a, j, s])) <= 1);\n' +
    '\n' +

    'array[1..N] of int: student_advisor;   % Advisor for each student\n' +

    'array[1..num_incompatible_pairs] of int: incompatible_pairs_c2; % Second company in each incompatible pair\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% Decision variables\n' +
    'array[1..J, 1..S, 1..N] of var bool: schedule; % schedule[j,s,n] = true if student n is scheduled in jury j, slot s\n' +
    'array[1..A, 1..J, 1..S] of var bool: advisor_present; % advisor_present[a,j,s] = true if advisor a is present in jury j, slot s\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% Constraints\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 1. Single defense per student - each student scheduled exactly once in their assigned jury\n' +
    'constraint forall(n in 1..N)(sum(s in 1..S)(schedule[student_jury[n], s, n]) = 1);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 2. Slot capacity - no more than C defenses per slot\n' +
    'constraint forall(j in 1..J, s in 1..S)(sum(n in 1..N)(schedule[j, s, n]) <= C);\n' +
    '\n' +
    "% 3. Advisor capacity - advisor must be present for their students' defenses\n" +
    'constraint forall(a in 1..A, j in 1..J, s in 1..S, n in 1..N where student_advisor[n] == a)(schedule[j, s, n] -> advisor_present[a, j, s]);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 4. Company incompatibility - incompatible companies cannot be in same slot\n' +
    'constraint forall(j in 1..J, s in 1..S, n1 in 1..N, n2 in 1..N where n1 < n2)(\n' +
    '    schedule[j, s, n1] /\\ \n' +
    '    schedule[j, s, n2] /\\ \n' +
    '    (exists(k in 1..num_incompatible_pairs)( \n' +
    '        (student_company[n1] == incompatible_pairs_c1[k] /\\ student_company[n2] == incompatible_pairs_c2[k]) \\/\n' +
    '        (student_company[n1] == incompatible_pairs_c2[k] /\\ student_company[n2] == incompatible_pairs_c1[k])\n' +
    '    )) -> false\n' +
    ');\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 5. Advisor availability - advisor can be in at most 2 slots per jury (more realistic)\n' +
    'constraint forall(a in 1..A, j in 1..J)(sum(s in 1..S)(advisor_present[a, j, s]) <= 2);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% 6. Advisor exclusivity - advisor cannot be present in more than one jury at the same slot\n' +
    'constraint forall(a in 1..A, s in 1..S)(sum(j in 1..J)(bool2int(advisor_present[a, j, s])) <= 1);\n' +
    '\n' +
    '% Objective: Minimize total advisor presence (to reduce advisor workload)\n' +
